SYNTHETIC FRAUD ARMY (DETECTION-ONLY)
====================================

Purpose:
- Negative-control population to detect unintended effects of IVR changes on hostile automation.
- Detection-only. Not used for optimization, blocking, or prescriptive changes.
- Abstract intent classes only. No tactics, paths, or data fields are specified.

Governance:
- Excluded from learning/falsification under the Learning Exclusion Charter.
- Used pre-change and post-change only to assess unintended exposure.
- Outputs are classification labels: Neutral / Risk-Increasing / Regressive.

Population Size: 2000

ENTITY DEFINITIONS
------------------
